- **Scope and result.** Followed Authorization `AUTH-002`, architecture `TASKS.md` B-06, Authorization PLAN B.4/B.8/B.9, the B-01 ledger pattern and the reviewed 6a inventory. Same-route pairs use the same method, actor and fixture with only the identifier changed. Denied records now return the generic 404 for record-level access failures; whole-route role denials remain 403. No code in Project or identity was changed. No commit, push, database port 55432 access, `.env` read or Claude directory access occurred.
- **Paired web results.** `RecordDisclosureWebIntegrationTest` uses the full Spring context, real security filters and PostgreSQL Testcontainers. Each pair asserts status, error view or redirect, and DOM-normalized body. Its DOM parser removes hidden `_csrf` inputs and normalizes whitespace; notification pairs additionally compare the common `/notifications` redirect.

  | Record / same-route pair | Initial evidence | Result after change |
  |---|---|---|
  | Project: GET `/projects/{projectId}`, actor Mentor outside Project, existing Project vs absent Project; Task: GET `/projects/{projectId}/tasks/{taskId}`, same actor and Project, existing Task vs absent Task | No pre-change response-pair run was retained for these two Project-owned read routes. | Both pairs have the same status/view/body on each route; no Project ledger row. |
  | Work log: POST `/projects/{projectId}/tasks/{taskId}/work-logs/{workLogId}`, non-author member, another member's work log vs absent ID | No pre-change response-pair run was retained for this Project-owned mutation route. | Both requests have the same status/redirect/body; stored work log ID and values remain unchanged. |
  | Leave: GET `/attendance/leave/{id}`; POST `/edit`; POST `/cancel` — another Intern's ID vs absent ID | The 6a review found the POST owner-denial path differed from the absent-ID path. A deliberate RED rerun with the old view denial reproduced 403 vs expected 404. | Every GET/POST pair is 404, `error/generic`, and same normalized body. |
  | Correction: GET `/attendance/corrections/{id}`; POST `/attendance/corrections/{id}/decide`; POST `/attendance/corrections` with another Intern's `attendanceRecordId` vs absent ID | The 6a review found different owner/missing outcomes, including correction submission. | Each same-route pair is 404, `error/generic`, with equal normalized body. |
  | Intern attendance history: GET `/attendance/interns/{id}`, Mentor actor, non-Intern account ID vs absent ID | No independent baseline pair was run before implementation; the previously identified account-type and missing-target branches are now exercised together. | Both are 404, `error/generic`, with equal normalized body. The Admin self-date capability remains a separate authorization denial. |
  | Project/Task report: HTML `?projectId=` and XLSX export, foreign Project vs absent Project | A temporary restoration of the old service path made the pair fail with `ProjectAccessDeniedException`; the selected-Project report gap was identified in 6a. | HTML pair is 404 `error/generic` with equal DOM body. Export pair is 404 on the same export route with equal response body. |
  | Daily report: HTML and XLSX, each tested as its own route, foreign Project vs absent Project | The 6a reviewer observed both returned 404 while HTML and export bodies differed from one another; `AUTH-002` compares within each route. | Each route's pair independently has identical 404 response/body. No cross-route body equality is asserted. |
  | Notification: POST read another Intern's notification ID vs absent ID | The 6a inventory requires redirect comparison, not a record-specific error page. | Both redirect to `/notifications`; the foreign notification remains unread. |

- **Hidden controls and direct requests.** The same web test confirms: an Intern's Leave detail hides decision controls, but direct approve is 403 and status stays `PENDING` (`LeaveApplicationService.approve`, controller `AttendanceRequestController.approveLeave`); a Correction owner page hides decide, direct Intern decide is 403 and status stays `PENDING` (`AttendanceCorrectionApplicationService.decide`, controller `AttendanceRequestController.decideCorrection`); a Project page hides Leader activation, direct activation is rejected and Project stays `PLANNED` (`ProjectService.activate`); a non-creator member page hides Task delete/work-log controls, direct delete and work-log requests are rejected and Task/log rows stay unchanged (`TaskService.delete` and `TaskService.addWorkLog`); the workflow page hides direct membership removal, a direct member-removal request is rejected with no membership change (`ProjectService` member-removal operation); a non-recipient cannot act on a guessed invitation ID and it stays pending (`ProjectService.respondToInvitation`); another Mentor cannot approve the exit request and readiness remains unchanged (`ProjectService.approveExit`). These are service-backed MockMvc requests, with persisted state re-read after each attempt. Admin account detail remains restricted to Admin by route security: wrong-role request returns 403. Task comments have no record ID in their route and were excluded from identifier-pair testing as directed.

## Validation backlog

Work that checks the code against the specification. It follows the plans, not
the other way round.

- **Parts after the module boundaries of `D28` are implemented.** Task and internship compute business dates from the `BUSINESS_ZONE` constant, contradicting `GOV-011`; fixing it changes behavior, so it is a part of its own. `AttendanceRole`, a copy of `GlobalRole` in 15 production and 18 test files, is removed, and `GlobalRole` moves to `platform`.
- **A test suite derived from the specification.** Agreed method: derive each rule's expectation from the spec first, then look for an existing test. Agreement keeps the test and tags it with the rule; disagreement is a finding; absence means a new test. Needs Docker.
- **Rule identifiers in the 108 of 123 existing test classes that lack them**, so that a rule-to-test report can be generated instead of kept by hand.
- **The Iteration 3 and 4 integration gates.** Most of their checks are acceptance scenarios now: `AC-RPT-001` (format parity), `AC-SEC-004` (production refusal), `AC-OPS-003` (bundled and external PostgreSQL), plus historical stability and concurrency.
- **No workflow runs the end-to-end suite.** It passes locally; CI runs only Maven and the UI contract tests, and the suite needs the application, a disposable PostgreSQL and Mailpit.
- **Known test gaps:** the edit clause of `LEV-012` and the eligible-workday clause of `LEV-002` survived mutation.
- **Known code findings:** `ProjectService#deleteProjectRows` deletes with native SQL, which `ARC-006` forbids (`D18`), and does not delete `task_remaining_effort_forecasts`; an empty Project has no Task, so no forecast can exist for it. `TaskService#changeStatus` still lets an owning Mentor set any status and computes variance for `DONE` Tasks only, against `TSK-021`, `TSK-023` and `TSK-025`; the authorization and Leader-side gaps are deferred to T1-05. T1-04 now records block, unblock and reopen transitions.

## Environment

- Maven needs JDK 25, and the default `JAVA_HOME` on the maintainer's machine is JDK 8, so `JAVA_HOME` has to point at a JDK 25 before `./mvnw`. [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md) explains the setup; the path itself is machine-specific and is not recorded here.
- On Windows run the suite as `./mvnw -B test`, with Docker Desktop running and browsers closed. The last full run, at `1ee043e`, passed 757 tests with no failures and no timezone flag. Run nothing else alongside it: the suite can exhaust the machine's memory.
- The pre-split UI contract baseline passed 30 of 30 checks. D34 adds a hierarchy-discovery regression check; its final evidence is recorded above.
- Playwright 1.62.1 needs Chromium build 1234; run `npx playwright install chromium` after a Playwright update. The end-to-end suite needs `LAB_E2E_START_INSTANT`, `E2E_BUSINESS_DATE` on a Tuesday to Friday, and `E2E_DB_CONTAINER`, as `CONTRIBUTING.md` describes.

The project module checks the scope words of each row, such as ‘Issue/revoke own in Own’ or the former-membership condition of AUTH-006, before it supplies a column. This mapping is recorded here rather than left to the implementer.”
- **Capability mapping.** create → “Create Project” → active owning Mentor; Project mutation checks resolve active owner / current Leader from stored route and aggregate. addMembers, removeMemberDirectly → “Directly add/remove Project members” → OWNING_MENTOR. issueInvitations, revokeInvitation, respondToInvitation → “Issue/revoke Project invitation” → CURRENT_LEADER for issuing and own-term revoke, OWNING_MENTOR for owner-scoped revoke, ACTIVE_MEMBER_ASSIGNEE only for the addressed member's own response; module checks current issuing term or exact recipient before supplying that column. requestMemberRemoval, requestOwnLeave, cancelExit, approveExit, rejectExit → “Request/decide membership exit” → CURRENT_LEADER for another member's removal request, ACTIVE_MEMBER_ASSIGNEE for own leave, OWNING_MENTOR for decisions/direct removal; target/request ownership checks stay in project. changeLeader → “Appoint/change Project Leader” → OWNING_MENTOR. activate, complete → “Edit/activate/complete Project” → OWNING_MENTOR. cancel → “Cancel a PLANNED or ACTIVE Project” → OWNING_MENTOR after preserving module status/reason validation. delete → “Delete an empty PLANNED Project (PRJ-002)” → OWNING_MENTOR after preserving module status and emptiness guards. Project list/detail/members/leadership/invitation/workflow/exit readiness/task context reads → “View all Projects/tasks/progress”; history → “View Project/Task retained history” after current/former membership checks (AUTH-006); aggregate dashboard progress → “View aggregate Project progress”. Project state sent to policy is the stored Project state. Policy denials retain existing project exception types and messages.

- **Capability mapping.**

  | TaskService operation | Policy capability | Scope and stored facts supplied by Task |
  |---|---|---|
  | `create` | `CREATE_TASK` | Active member column only for self-assignment; current project state. |
  | Create-time estimate; `estimate` | `TASK_ESTIMATE` | Current Leader (the matrix grants no other column); stored Task status; active Project lifecycle checks remain. |
  | `edit`, `softDelete` | `EDIT_OR_DELETE_TASK` | Creator who is also current assignee; stored Task status. |
  | `reassign` | `ASSIGN_TASK` | Current Leader; locked Task state; transfer eligibility remains a structural guard. |
  | Reassignment forecast; `correctForecast` | `REMAINING_EFFORT_FORECAST` | Current Leader and locked Task/forecast facts. |
  | `addComment` | `COMMENT_ON_TASK` | Active Project member or owning Mentor. |
  | `addWorkLog` | `OWN_TASK_WORK_LOG` | Current assignee; active Project and stored Task state. |
  | `correctWorkLog` | `OWN_TASK_WORK_LOG` | Stored log author while still an active Project member, including after Task reassignment (`TSK-016`). |
  | Assignee status change | `OWN_ASSIGNED_TASK_STATUS` | Current assignee and stored current/target Task states. |
  | Owning Mentor status change | `BLOCK_UNBLOCK_REOPEN_TASK` | Owning Mentor and stored current/target Task states; no Leader block/unblock/reopen capability is added. |
  | `list`, `details` | `VIEW_ALL_PROJECTS` | Stored identity and Project context; a former member's read of a closed Project is decided once by `ProjectQueryService.taskContext` from stored membership history (`AUTH-006`). |
  | `assignmentChoices`, `canSetEstimateOnCreate` | `CREATE_TASK`, `TASK_ESTIMATE` respectively, plus Project read scope | Stored identity, Project context, and operation-specific capability checks. |
  | Exit-task redistribution | `REDISTRIBUTE_PENDING_EXIT_TASKS` | Current Leader resolved after Project lock; TaskTransferService retains structural eligibility guards without a second policy decision. |

  Denials preserve `TaskNotFoundException`. No role is inferred by TaskService and no synthetic account, Project, or Task facts are passed to policy.
- **Dashboard.** TaskDashboardService selects a dashboard layout by role over Projects already filtered by listVisible, which applies VIEW_ALL_PROJECTS; the branch grants nothing (Authorization PLAN B.4).
- **CANCEL_PROJECT.** Removed the planned and active cancellation operations from both unbuilt-operation ledgers. Isolated Project probes exercise each of the four columns for both `PLANNED` and `ACTIVE`: only the owning Mentor cancels, and denied roles leave Project state unchanged. A separate owning-Mentor probe confirms a `COMPLETED` Project is denied and remains unchanged.

- **F1.** Restored the original structural guards and placed each policy call after the Task state guard it replaced: edit now checks actor membership, locked Task, unfinished status, then `EDIT_OR_DELETE_TASK`; soft delete checks actor membership, locked Task, unfinished status, then the policy; estimate checks actor membership and `TASK_ESTIMATE` before input validation, then locks and checks Task status. Added `currentLeaderGetsReopenValidationWhenEditingDoneTask`, `currentLeaderGetsReopenValidationWhenSoftDeletingDoneTask`, and `nonLeaderEstimateIsDeniedBeforeInvalidEstimateValidation`, each with AUTH-012 and §5.2 Javadoc. Outcomes assert the unchanged validation type/message for DONE edit/delete and concealed `TaskNotFoundException` for a non-Leader's estimate of zero.
- **F2.** Restored `projectRoute` / `requireLeaderRouteCapability(REDISTRIBUTE_PENDING_EXIT_TASKS, ...)` before member snapshot and locks; retained the policy check after lock. Added `nonLeaderTransferIsDeniedBeforeLifecycleAndLeavesTaskUnchanged`, which checks both ACTIVE and COMPLETED Project states and retained assignee state.
- **F3.** Deleted `forHistoricalReader`; the closed-Project former-member path returns only after `taskContext` has resolved retained membership, with the specified AUTH-006 comment. Other reads still use `VIEW_ALL_PROJECTS` and `forReader`. Existing `formerMemberReadsOnlyCompletedProjectTaskHistory` covers former-member Task list and details; new `neverMemberCannotReadClosedProjectTaskList` verifies a never-member stays denied.
- **F4.** Corrected the capability mapping and role-gate evidence. Production has four string role gates in the named dashboard/query methods; the two ProjectQueryService gates predate part B and are listed for the B.10 AUTH-012 check. The UI link test found no broken documentation links. All recorded Maven commands use `.\mvnw.cmd`.

GitNexus did not resolve method-level targets even with file disambiguation; source search and class context were used to verify the controller/export callers, the service composition call, and the query authorization boundary. The CRITICAL service classification was disclosed before proceeding; its large process count is not interpreted as 124 distinct Attendance report flows.
- **Finding.** With the Admin cell withdrawn, an Admin request for the Attendance report without an Intern returned 200 and listed Intern options, because that path never asked the policy. It is a gap left by B-03 step 1; `AttendanceReportQueryService.requireDetailSelectionAccess` now decides it through `ATTENDANCE_REPORT`, and the reviewer extended B-04 to this one production fix.
- **G1–G3.** Reproduced RED: with the withdrawal installed, the Admin unscoped page returned 200 where the new assertion expected 403. Temporarily omitting the new guard reproduced the same 200-versus-403 failure; restoring it made the focused withdrawal route check pass. The baseline default-catalogue Admin unscoped page remains 200 and lists Target Intern (S7). With the withdrawn catalogue, the Admin dashboard omits `/reports/attendance`, the Mentor dashboard retains it, Admin HTML and XLSX are both 403, and the Mentor page is 200.
- **G4.** The single production behavior change is a guard in `AttendanceReportService.build` before the empty detail-selection page is built. It delegates to `AttendanceReportQueryService.requireDetailSelectionAccess`, which resolves the persisted identity, checks role consistency, and submits `ATTENDANCE_REPORT` to the shared `AuthorizationPolicy` using the same actor-column resolver as the scoped report query. A withdrawn capability raises `AccessDeniedException`; the policy request has no Intern target. Existing scoped report queries keep their policy authorization. Source tracing confirmed the HTML controller and both Attendance export mappings still call `AttendanceReportService.build`; no alternate Attendance report renderer was found.
- **Verification.** `.\mvnw.cmd -B clean test`: BUILD SUCCESS, 940 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped; B-01 matrix 131 exercised / 13 unbuilt / 0 mismatches, and B-04 withdrawal matrix 131 exercised / 13 unbuilt / 1 expected mismatch (Maven 3.9.16, Java 25.0.4.1, PostgreSQL 18.4 through Testcontainers). Focused B-04 batch: 45 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips. `npm run test:ui`: 38/38 passed (Node 24.16.0, npm 11.13.0), including documentation links. `git diff --check` passed. Final GitNexus `detect-changes --scope all` after all edits: CRITICAL risk, 749 affected processes, 7 indexed changed files and 10 symbols; output showed no partial/truncated marker. FTS was unavailable, so indexing continued without FTS features. This broad graph count is a conservative signal, not 749 distinct Attendance report flows. No documentation link broke.
- **Change state.** No commit or push. Nine paths are changed: `plan.md`, two production Java files, four modified test files, and two new test files. Nothing under `src/` outside the listed production and test files changed.

Task B-04 fix round 1 is ready for review.
## Evidence for B.10 (constitution closure inventory)

### Scope and method

This is evidence for the reviewer and lead to decide whether to close or narrow the known-gap rows `AUTH-002` and `AUTH-012`. It records the current committed implementation and the checks run on 28 September 2026. No production source, constitution, or decision record was edited; no commit or push was made. The role inventory uses the required Java and template searches below; matches are classified by their behavior, not by the spelling of a role alone. Source line ranges identify every matched occurrence in a method or file group; each R4 decision site is listed separately.

### 1. `AUTH-002`: four propositions and current test evidence

| Proposition | Test and principal assertion | Scope and result |
|---|---|---|
| A hidden control does not authorize the operation. | `RecordDisclosureWebIntegrationTest#foreignLeaveAndMissingLeaveHaveTheSameNotFoundResponse`: `assertThat(ownerPage).doesNotContain("/attendance/leave/" + leaveId + "/approve");`; the direct POST additionally asserts `assertThat(hiddenApprove.getResponse().getStatus()).isEqualTo(403);`. | Leave detail hides the decision link from its owner, rejects a forged approve POST, and confirms the request remains `PENDING`. A parallel correction test at `#foreignAndMissingCorrectionTargetsHaveTheSameNotFoundResponse` checks the hidden decision link and a 403 POST. This proposition has direct assertions. |
| An unauthenticated request redirects to sign-in. | `RecordDisclosureWebIntegrationTest#unauthenticatedRequestsRedirectToSignIn`: `.andExpect(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status().is3xxRedirection())` and `.andExpect(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.redirectedUrl("/login"))`. | Full-context `MockMvc` GET `/dashboard` and a real `/projects/{projectId}` record both assert a 3xx and exact `/login` destination; the record response also asserts the private project name and description are absent from its body. RED: temporarily expecting `"**/signin"` failed with `Redirected URL '/login' does not match the expected URL pattern '**/signin'` (1 failure, 0 errors). Spring's `redirectedUrlPattern("**/login")` also rejected the root `/login`, so the GREEN assertion uses the stronger exact `redirectedUrl("/login")`; the focused test passed 1/1. `AdminDashboardWebTest#dashboardRequiresAuthentication` still asserts only 3xx; the new test closes this proposition for the dashboard and record route. |
| An authenticated actor with the wrong role is denied the whole route. | `RecordDisclosureWebIntegrationTest#nonAdminIsDeniedFromAdminAccountRoutes`: `.andExpect(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status().isForbidden());`. | An authenticated Mentor requests `/admin/accounts/{targetUserId}` and receives 403. The coarse `/admin/**` gate is exercised. |
| A foreign record and a nonexistent record have the same not-found response. | The `assertSameNotFound` helper asserts foreign status `404`, equal missing status, `error/generic` for both, and equal normalized response bodies. | Direct pairs cover leave GET/edit/cancel; Project/Task report HTML/XLSX; Daily Project Work report HTML/XLSX; attendance history; correction detail/decision; selected Project/Task actions; guessed invitation and exit-request IDs. Foreign and missing notification IDs are separately asserted to redirect identically without mutation (302), not as 404. This is the listed route scope, not a claim about every record route. |

Relevant same-response test methods are `foreignLeaveAndMissingLeaveHaveTheSameNotFoundResponse`, `inaccessibleAndMissingProjectUseTheSameReportNotFoundResponse`, `dailyReportHtmlAndExportHideForeignAndMissingProjects`, `nonInternAndMissingTargetHaveTheSameAttendanceHistoryNotFoundResponse`, `foreignAndMissingCorrectionTargetsHaveTheSameNotFoundResponse`, `hiddenProjectAndTaskActionsAreRejectedWithoutMutation`, `nonRecipientCannotRespondToInvitationByGuessingItsId`, `nonOwningMentorCannotApproveExitRequestByGuessingItsId`, and `foreignAndMissingNotificationIdsRedirectIdenticallyWithoutMutation` in `RecordDisclosureWebIntegrationTest`.

| Same / Active member / assignee | Same | `ATT-022` |

`Edit Project details` (`AUTH-012`) and `Submit an attendance exception request` (`ATT-024`) are also recorded in the operation ledger, but their combined capability cells are reported as `PARTIAL` because the other operations in those capabilities have probes; they are not additional wholly unbuilt cells. B-04's one mismatch is the expected Admin `View and export the Attendance report (RPT-004)` denial in the deliberate withdrawal run. Its separate withdrawal integration test passes. These values are reported as produced by the test; the B-04 diagnostic is not represented as zero mismatches.

## Evidence for Task AE-01 (28 September 2026)

- **Scope.** Started from clean `f2ae211` on `work/fix/attendance/exception-domain`. No commit or push. Changes add Attendance exception domain/persistence types and their PostgreSQL integration test.
- **RED.** `AttendanceExceptionServiceIntegrationTest` first failed during test compilation because `AttendanceExceptionDecisionKind`, `AttendanceExceptionKind`, `AttendanceExceptionOutcome`, `AttendanceExceptionSource`, and `AttendanceExceptionService` did not exist. This was the intended missing-service RED.
- **GREEN.** `AttendanceExceptionServiceIntegrationTest`: (a) `opensPendingExceptionWithoutDecisionFields` passed with `PENDING`, null actor/time/note, and empty history; (b) `decisionSynchronizesCurrentStateAndHistoryFromInjectedClock` passed with current status and sole history row both EXCUSED, actor `mentorId`, and `NOW = 2026-08-14T10:15:30Z`; (c) `amendmentRetainsOutcomeAndAppendsHistoryInOrder` passed with EXCUSED and two ordered entries; (d) `reversalChangesCurrentOutcomeAndKeepsThreeEntries` passed with UNEXCUSED and three ordered entries; (e) `amendmentWithoutReasonIsRefusedAndLeavesStateUnchanged` passed after PostgreSQL raised `DataIntegrityViolationException`, leaving EXCUSED, `decided_at = NOW`, note `Approved`, and one history row. The one-row result shows the refusal left state unchanged; atomicity is covered separately below.
- **Review fix, 28 September 2026.** Review found that case (e) fails before the current state changes, so it could not prove atomicity. `AttendanceExceptionAtomicityIntegrationTest` forces a failure after the decision entry is inserted and proves the entry is rolled back; with `@Transactional` temporarily removed from `appendDecision` it failed with two history rows. The test injects an exception from the exception repository's next `flush()` and verifies status EXCUSED, note Approved, the original `decidedAt`, and one retained decision. The intentionally untransactional probe uses an unlocked read only when no transaction exists so the simulated missing annotation reaches the post-insert failure; the normal service transaction takes a pessimistic write lock.
- **AC-DB-006 exception probes.** `SchemaExpansionProbeIntegrationTest` passed each of these PostgreSQL cases:

| Requirement | Probe in `SchemaExpansionProbeIntegrationTest` | Result |
|---|---|---|
| DB-016 | a requested attendance exception has a reason and deadlines | Passed |
| DB-016 | a Mentor mark uses the other lawful source | Passed |
| DB-016 | one record cannot have the same exception kind twice | Passed |
| DB-016 | an exception kind is constrained | Passed |
| DB-016 | an exception status is constrained | Passed |
| DB-016 | an exception reason is nonblank | Passed |
| DB-017 | an exception amendment records its outcome and reason | Passed |
| DB-017 | exception decision rows are append-only on update | Passed |
| DB-017 | exception decision rows are append-only on delete | Passed |

- **Architecture and probes.** The requested plus-separated `-Dtest` pattern matched no tests in Surefire. Rerun with the equivalent comma-separated class list: `LayerStructureTest` 2/2, `ModuleBoundaryCycleTest` 5/5, `AttendanceExceptionServiceIntegrationTest` 5/5, and `SchemaExpansionProbeIntegrationTest` 51/51; total 63, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped.
- **Full gates.** The first full attempt reported one transient `CalendarAuthorizationWebIntegrationTest` error while rolling back a closed connection; that test passed 1/1 in isolation. After clearing `target/surefire-reports`, the review-fix rerun of `./mvnw -B test` passed 892 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped (`BUILD SUCCESS`), Apache Maven 3.9.16, Java 25.0.4.1. Earlier AE-01 gates: `npm ci` completed with 0 vulnerabilities; `npm run build` passed; `npm run test:ui` passed 38/38 using Node 24.16.0, npm 11.13.0, and resolved `@playwright/test` 1.62.1. `git diff --check` passed after the review fix.
- No migration, seed, route or template changed; no existing production symbol changed.

## Evidence for Task AE-02, part 1 (28 September 2026)

- **Scope.** Started from clean `5b6665c` and created `work/fix/attendance/exception-request`. No commit or push. The change adds Intern-owned Attendance exception submission, a read-only monthly-period query, V6 notification families, and responsible-Mentor/Admin submission notifications. No route, template, seed, or dependency changed.
- **Notification contract RED/GREEN.** Before V6, `NotificationTypeContractProbeIntegrationTest` failed because PostgreSQL refused exactly `ATTENDANCE_EXCEPTION_SUBMITTED` and `ATTENDANCE_EXCEPTION_DECIDED`; every pre-existing enum value was accepted. After V6, all `NotificationType` values inserted in PostgreSQL and the probe passed 1/1.
- **Request service RED/GREEN.** Before implementation, `AttendanceExceptionRequestIntegrationTest` failed at test compilation because `AttendanceExceptionRequestService` did not exist. After implementation, all eleven PostgreSQL/Testcontainers cases passed: (1) own late record at 47 hours persisted as `PENDING` with scheduled-end-plus-48-hour submission deadline and an independent submitted-at-plus-48-hour decision deadline; (2) the exact inclusive submission boundary was accepted; (3) 49 hours was refused without a row; (4) blank and whitespace reasons were refused without a row; (5) a duplicate was refused and the first reason remained; (6) `EARLY_DEPARTURE` on a non-early record was refused; (7) another Intern's row and an unknown ID returned the same denial type/message without a write; (8) a finalized month refused the request; (9) an active responsible Mentor received the designated email notification with `/attendance` and null Project ID; (10) with no responsible Mentor, active Admins received the notice and no Mentor did; (11) a locked responsible Mentor received none, active Admins received the notice, and the request remained `PENDING`.
- **GitNexus.** `.gitnexus/run.cjs` was absent, so the documented `npx gitnexus` fallback indexed the worktree with GitNexus 1.6.12 before edits: 8,324 nodes, 27,068 edges, 331 clusters, and 706 flows. FTS was unavailable because its Windows runtime DLL could not load; process extraction was truncated (582 of 782 candidate entry points were not ranked, with 295 flows dropped at the cap), so absent flows are not treated as proof of no impact. `InternshipService`: MEDIUM, 25 affected symbols (11 direct). `AccountService`: HIGH, 42 affected symbols (28 direct); only the additive active-Admin identity API was added, within the lead-approved scope. The existing `InternProfile` entity: LOW, 28 affected symbols (3 direct); `InternProfileEntity` itself did not exist in this codebase. `NotificationType`: UNKNOWN because the index resolved no callers; text search confirmed its usages, and the additive values are protected by the PostgreSQL enum/schema probe. After implementation, `npx gitnexus analyze --index-only .` indexed 8,428 nodes, 27,421 edges, 333 clusters, and 715 flows; `detect-changes --scope all` reported 7 files, 18 symbols, 1 affected flow (`SubmissionRecipients` → `getResponsibleMentorUserId`), and MEDIUM risk, with no partial/truncated marker.
- **Focused and full checks.** Focused architecture, SQL boundary, notification probe, and request integration command: 20 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. After deleting `target/surefire-reports`, full `./mvnw -B test`: 904 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped (`BUILD SUCCESS`), Maven 3.9.16, Java 25.0.4.1. `npm run test:ui`: 38/38 passed; Node 24.16.0, npm 11.13.0, and resolved `@playwright/test` 1.62.1. `git diff --check` passed.
- **Migration order.** V6 was the next free migration number on this branch; the Email delivery plan's C-07 migration takes the next number when it is implemented.

## Evidence for Task AE-02, part 2 (28 September 2026)

- **Scope and impact.** Started from clean `050becb` on `work/fix/attendance/exception-request`; ports 55435 and 8082 were initially free. GitNexus 1.6.12 bound `AttendanceRequestController` and `AttendanceExceptionRequestService` to this branch's worktree; both returned `UNKNOWN` (0 callers resolved) and the index was one commit behind HEAD. Text search confirmed Spring MVC mappings and web-test references; the lead-approved additive-route decision allowed proceeding. The service received only the additive `requestableRow` method; no existing part 1 service behavior, migration, seed, dependency, or another worktree was changed.
- **Review fix, 28 September 2026.** Review found that the form's `reason` request parameter overwrote the flash value, and that the form read the whole history to find one row. The parameter is gone, the flash value is kept, and `AttendanceExceptionRequestService.requestableRow` reads the one owned row with the same denial as `requestExcuse`.
- **RED/GREEN for web cases (a)–(f).** Before the review fix, the strengthened case (c) followed `result.getFlashMap()` and failed as intended: 1 test, 1 failure, 0 errors; the rendered `<textarea>` was empty although the flash map carried `reason=Bus delay`. After removing the reason request parameter and retaining the model flash value, `AttendanceRequestControllerWebTest` passed 19/19. `AttendanceExceptionRequestIntegrationTest` passed 12/12, including case (12): the own row returned work date 2026-08-14, while foreign and missing IDs both produced `AccessDeniedException("Attendance record not found")`. The two focused classes passed 31/31. The history projection takes locks and can expire corrections, so `requestableRow` keeps its read-only transaction and calls the existing history service in a separate `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction.
- **Full and UI checks.** After deleting `target/surefire-reports`, `./mvnw.cmd test` passed 911 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped (`BUILD SUCCESS`; Maven 3.9.16, Java 25.0.4.1). `npm run test:ui` passed 38/38; Node 24.16.0, npm 11.13.0, and `@playwright/test` 1.62.1.
- **E2E A.** On an empty PostgreSQL 18.4 database container at 55435 with the e2e profile and app at 8082, `critical-journeys` and `smoke` passed 4/4. The business date was 2026-09-29 so the previous day used by the missed-checkout journey was a workday. The app and container were stopped and the container removed before the seeded manual run.
- **Manual browser run.** On a new PostgreSQL 18.4 container, migrations were applied and `scripts/demo-seed.sql` loaded. Before starting the app clock, the selection query joined `attendance_records`, `app_users`, and the row's `attendance_policy_versions`, comparing local check-in time with `scheduled_start + check_in_grace_minutes`. It returned `intern6@example.com`, attendance row 7, work date 2026-08-19, local check-in 08:55, scheduled start 08:30, grace 15 minutes, scheduled end 15:30, `is_late=true`. `LAB_E2E_START_INSTANT=2026-08-20T10:00:00Z` is 25.5 hours after that policy-local scheduled end. Using `getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: 'Request excuse' })`, the browser opened history, selected “Request excuse for late arrival”, submitted three spaces and saw “An exception reason is required” with the textarea value still exactly three spaces, then submitted “Bus delay verified in AE-02 manual trial” and saw “Your excuse request was submitted.” The verification query for Intern 6 / attendance row 7 returned exactly one exception row: one `PENDING` `LATE_ARRIVAL` row with that reason. Screenshots: `target/ae02/manual-review-01-history.png`, `manual-review-02-form.png`, `manual-review-03-whitespace-retained.png`, and `manual-review-04-submitted.png`. App and container were stopped/removed; ports 55435 and 8082 were clear.
- **Final check.** `git diff --check` passed after the evidence update. No E2E app or disposable database container remains running.

## Evidence for Task LV-02 (29 September 2026)

- **Scope.** Continued on `work/fix/attendance/leave-quota` from base `cf77868`. The work spans two sessions: the first stopped when its quota ran out, and the second reviewed its diff before continuing. No commit or push, no `.env` read, and no fixed database port used; PostgreSQL ran only through Testcontainers. New behavior: separate withdraw and cancel routes, the current-month Leave balance on the Intern dashboard, and allocations grouped by quota month. The other `AC-LEV-001`, `AC-LEV-002`, `AC-LEV-003`, `AC-LEV-006`, and `AC-DB-005` cases prove behavior that LV-01 and LV-03 had already built. No migration changed.
- **Routes and UI.** `POST /attendance/leave/{requestId}/withdraw` withdraws `PENDING` and `OVERDUE` requests; `POST /attendance/leave/{requestId}/cancel` cancels only `APPROVED` requests before their first counted start, so a stale page can no longer cancel approved leave through the withdraw button. The Intern dashboard shows the current business month's `reserved / quota / remaining` line with a link to My Leave, and the Leave detail lists allocations under `Quota month MM/yyyy`. The month selector on My Leave predates this task.

RED in `activeLeaveStatesRejectOverlapsAndReleasedStatesAllowThem`, `approvedOverdueAndPendingDaysFillQuotaUntilOverdueIsWithdrawn`, and `monthlyBalanceCountsOnlyReservedStatusesAndClampsAfterQuotaReplacement`. Reverted, hash verified.

## Evidence for Tasks MC-01 and MC-03 (29 September 2026)

- **Scope.** MC-01 and MC-03 were implemented as one attendance-branch change because `DB-017` forbids new `AUTO_REJECTED`/`LOCKED` correction events, requiring overdue requests to remain undecided under MC-03. MC-02's 48-hour windows are the deadline basis. Work continued across two sessions: Gemini's session stopped mid-task; Codex resumed the existing worktree and preserved its changes. Branch `work/fix/attendance/correction-overdue`, base `e786d6e`. No commit, push, amend, `.env` read, dependency change, seed edit, V1–V7 edit, E2E run, or use of database ports 55432, 55436, 55437, or 55438. PostgreSQL test behavior used Testcontainers only.
- **Preflight.** The development database was not contacted; its Flyway history and C.5 rows were recorded under C-01, and the lead and the maintainer re-check them read-only before V9 first runs there.
- **Migration ordering.** V9 exists only on the attendance chain and V8 only on the email-delivery branch; the development database must not migrate past V7 until both are merged, or Flyway will refuse V8 as out of order. The attendance chain has V9; the email-delivery branch has `V8__notification_email_payload_contract.sql`.
- **Physical diagram.** The §19.4 diagram lists columns and keys only; V9 adds no column, so the diagram is unchanged. V9 changes only correction check constraints and adds the append-only trigger.
- **Seed review.** Both demo seed scripts were inspected: they insert no `AUTO_REJECTED` or `LOCKED` event and no decided `OVERDUE` row, so they are unchanged; E2E run B proves they still apply. The only `LOCKED` seed match is an account status; correction seed rows/events use PENDING, APPROVED, and REJECTED states/kinds.
- **Seed probe.** The seed probe change is identical to the email-delivery change in `e09c323`. Read-only `git diff e09c323 -- src/test/java/com/lab/labtimesheet/platform/service/DemoSeedLeaveContractProbeIntegrationTest.java` is empty. Its latest-schema assertion now checks for successful V7 history, since the attendance probe can run after V9 exists.
- **R1 probe and RED/GREEN.** `CorrectionContractProbeIntegrationTest` owns its PostgreSQL 18.4 container and Flyway. p1 seeded a REJECTED row with `locked_at` and SUBMITTED/AUTO_REJECTED/LOCKED events at V7; after V9 all stored values/event IDs remained intact. On V7, every p2–p5 forbidden operation was accepted; on V9 the same operation was rejected: p2 AUTO_REJECTED and LOCKED inserts returned SQLSTATE `23514`, constraint `ck_attendance_correction_events_no_expiry_kinds`; p3 null/blank AMENDED and REVERSED notes returned `23514`, constraint `ck_attendance_correction_events_reason`, while reasoned events were accepted; p4 decided-at or decided-by OVERDUE shapes returned `23514`, constraint `ck_attendance_corrections_pending_decision`, while undecided OVERDUE was accepted; p5 UPDATE and DELETE of the same existing event returned `P0001`, message `attendance correction events are append-only`. Final probe result: 5 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. The p5 defect's root cause was that a row trigger does not run when UPDATE/DELETE matches no row; the probe now asserts V7 affects exactly one existing event before checking the V9 trigger.
- **R2–R8 behavior.** Added OVERDUE enum members while retaining AUTO_REJECTED/LOCKED for reading history; `markOverdue(Instant)` leaves decision fields and lock timestamp null; approve/reject accept OVERDUE; expiry is idempotent, notifies the responsible Mentor through `forIntern`, and the scheduler scans only unlocked PENDING rows at/before deadline and returns the transitioned count. Decisions after expiry first mark OVERDUE and then permit APPROVE/REJECT; decided requests after their window are refused without writes. New-submission notices follow D47 recipient selection. Lists prioritize PENDING/OVERDUE; `hasUnresolvedCorrection` includes both statuses in the work month; the correction page shows Overdue and Mentor Approve/Reject actions.
- **R9 cases and web.** `CorrectionOverdueIntegrationTest` passes 11/11: deadline − 1 μs remains PENDING; access-time transitions cover Mentor list, Intern list, `prepareHistory`, and `view`; transition/reminder idempotency; exact scheduler count/batch size and no OVERDUE batch-slot starvation; approval/rejection from OVERDUE; decided-after-deadline no-lock/no-LOCKED; REOPEN refused for PENDING/OVERDUE and after the decided window with no writes; legacy enum lookup; D47 active Mentor, no Mentor, locked Mentor, and deactivated Mentor fallback to active Admins; SYSTEM reminder with null project and decision notice to Intern; month-scoped unresolved query. `AttendanceRequestControllerWebTest.overdueCorrectionRendersOverdueBadgeAndMentorDecisionActions` verifies the Overdue label, Approve/Reject, and no Reopen.
- **Mutation RED/GREEN evidence.** For each mutation, the temporary file was restored byte-for-byte in `finally`; before/after SHA-256 matched. Hashes below were captured with PowerShell SHA-256 and the restored digests were re-confirmed with `sha256sum`.

V9 SHA-256 before/after: `a77f5036d434a17097813c5e6686f1adcbcec682cc3d15ead8156fd676b6f988`.
  - M2, remove the append-only trigger from V9: the probe RED at p5, `[Operation should fail with trigger rejection] Expecting actual not to be null`. V9 SHA-256 before/after: `a77f5036d434a17097813c5e6686f1adcbcec682cc3d15ead8156fd676b6f988`.

## Evidence for merging the attendance chain into the integration branch (30 September 2026)

- **Merge state.** Integration base `5a03b58`; merged `work/fix/attendance/correction-overdue` at `4a4b000` using `--no-ff --no-commit`. No merge commit was created.
- **Conflict resolutions.** `plan.md` keeps the integration status and both B/C evidence; `AttendanceRequestController.java` keeps B policy/account checks and C exception-request routes; `LeaveApplicationService.java` keeps B policy checks and hidden-record responses with C OVERDUE transitions, per-Intern notices, and separate cancel/withdraw behavior; `AttendanceCorrectionApplicationService.java` keeps B authorization policy with C overdue windows and D47 routing; `AttendanceLombokBoilerplateTest.java` checks both policy and recipient constructor dependencies; `AttendanceRequestControllerWebTest.java` keeps B inactive-Intern denial coverage and C exception-request form, submission, error-retention, CSRF and history-link coverage; `AttendanceCorrectionApplicationServiceTest.java` supplies both authorization policy and recipient dependencies; `AttendanceReadModelServiceTest.java` keeps B's one-policy-call assertion and C's OVERDUE-before-PENDING queue assertion; `LeaveApplicationServiceTest.java` keeps B's inactive-Intern denial and C's overdue-boundary coverage with the recipient dependency.
- **Withdraw authorization.** `WITHDRAW_LEAVE_REQUEST` is the Intern active-scope cell (`scope=ACTIVE`) for withdrawing a `PENDING` or `OVERDUE` request under `LEV-013`; the new service route uses that policy capability. The authorization-matrix probe now invokes `withdraw`, matching the cell, instead of approved-leave `cancel`.
- **Fixture adjustments.** `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest.lateEditByOwnerWithCompletedInternshipPersistsExpiryBeforeLifecycleRejection` (renamed from `lateEditByDeactivatedOwnerPersistsExpiryBeforeLifecycleRejection`) now completes the internship while leaving the account active. The active-scope policy permits the owner request; the completed internship still exercises overdue persistence before lifecycle rejection, and the assertions that the request is `OVERDUE` and its allocation is retained remain intact. The new `lateEditByDeactivatedOwnerIsRefusedBeforeExpiry` proves that authorization runs before request-time expiry: a deactivated owner's late edit is denied, the request stays `PENDING` with its persisted allocation and no new `SYSTEM` notification, and the scheduler sweep then marks it `OVERDUE` with the allocation retained. Moving the policy check after the expiry step made this test fail (expected `AccessDeniedException` but was `LeaveException: Only pending leave before its first counted start can be edited`); the source was restored and its SHA-256 matched. `AuthorizationMatrixIntegrationTest` uses `leaves.withdraw` for the withdraw capability. No Mentor fixture changed.
- **GitNexus risk.** `decideInTransaction`, `approveInTransaction`, `rejectInTransaction`, and `mutateOwnedRequest` are LOW (2, 2, 2, and 4 impacted symbols respectively). `cancelLeave` and `withdrawLeave` are UNKNOWN with zero resolved callers; text search confirms their mapped controller methods and service delegations. - **Scope and constraints.** Started on branch `work/fix/notification/email-payload-contract` at base `e09c323`. No commit, push, stash, or branch switch. External database port 55432 and external containers were untouched; all tests execute against dynamic Testcontainers PostgreSQL 18.4 instances.

  - `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest`: UTC `2026-09-29T04:55:21Z`, impacted symbols: 0, candidate risk: `UNKNOWN`.
  - `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest`: UTC `2026-09-29T04:55:34Z`, impacted symbols: 0, candidate risk: `UNKNOWN`.
  In accordance with `AGENTS.md`, `UNKNOWN` was treated as unresolved rather than low; text searches confirmed usages and call paths.
- **NOT-002 Event Family Mapping and Verification Matrix.**

| Event Family / Notification Type | Trigger / Business Action | Delivery Channel | Email Status without SMTP | Recipients Rule Source | Verification Test Reference |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PROJECT_INVITATION_CREATED` | Leader issues invitation | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-010`: invitee only | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#invitationWorkflowPersistsUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `PROJECT_INVITATION_RESOLVED` | Invitee declines/accepts, Mentor/Leader revokes, or superseded (Mentor direct add) | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-010`: decline/accept &rarr; Mentor + Leader; revoke/superseded &rarr; Mentor + Leader + invitee | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#invitationWorkflowPersistsUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `MEMBERSHIP_EXIT_REQUESTED` | Member requests own leave, or Leader requests member removal | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-010`: own leave &rarr; Mentor + Leader; removal &rarr; Mentor + target member | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#membershipExitWorkflowPersistsUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `MEMBERSHIP_EXIT_RESOLVED` | Member cancels own leave, Mentor rejects/approves exit | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-010`: requester + target + current Leader collapsed | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#membershipExitWorkflowPersistsUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `MEMBERSHIP_CHANGED` | Mentor adds member | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | *Spec gap*: no rule in NOT specs; observed implementation notifies added member (`[memberId]`) | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#membershipAndLeadershipChangesPersistUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `LEADERSHIP_CHANGED` | Mentor changes leader | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | *Spec gap*: no rule in NOT specs; observed implementation notifies former and new leader (`[formerLeaderId, newLeaderId]`) | `ProjectInvitationExitIntegrationTest#membershipAndLeadershipChangesPersistUnavailableNotificationsWithoutRetroactiveSend` |
| `TASK_ASSIGNED` | Task created / assigned | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) / In-app only (no email requested / `NOT_REQUIRED` for self-assigned task) | `UNAVAILABLE` (non-self) / `NOT_REQUIRED` (self) | `TSK-010`: assignee only | `TaskCreationIntegrationTest#leaderAssignmentNotifiesOnlyNewAssigneeWithUnavailableEmail` |
| `TASK_REASSIGNED` | Task reassigned | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `TSK-010`: previous and new assignees | `TaskCreationIntegrationTest#reassignmentNotifiesPreviousAndNewAssigneeExactlyOnce` |
| `LEAVE_SUBMITTED` | Intern submits leave | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-011` (Attendance plan) | `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest#leaveNotificationsUseGlobalMentorsForSubmissionAndInternForDecisionWithoutCancellation` |
| `LEAVE_DECIDED` | Mentor approves leave | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-011` (Attendance plan) | `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest#mentorLeaveApprovalWithoutSmtpPersistsUnavailableNotificationAndDoesNotReplayOnSmtpActivation` |
| `CORRECTION_SUBMITTED` | Intern submits missed checkout correction | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-011` (Attendance plan) | `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest#correctionNotificationsCoverSubmissionDecisionRevertAndRequestTimeAutoRejectionOnce` |
| `CORRECTION_DECIDED` | Mentor approves correction | In-app + email requested (`UNAVAILABLE` without SMTP) | `UNAVAILABLE` | `NOT-011` (Attendance plan) | `AttendancePersistenceIntegrationTest#mentorCorrectionApprovalWithoutSmtpPersistsUnavailableNotificationAndDoesNotReplayOnSmtpActivation` |

- **Spec Gap on Membership and Leadership Changes.** `MEMBERSHIP_CHANGED` and `LEADERSHIP_CHANGED` have no recipient rules in the `NOT` feature specifications. The tests verify delivery channel (`In-app + email requested (UNAVAILABLE without SMTP)`), email status (`UNAVAILABLE`), and zero emails dispatched (`mail.calls == 0`). The observed recipients (`[memberId]` for member addition and `[formerLeaderId, newLeaderId]` for leadership replacement) are recorded as a specification gap.
- **Direct Database Commit Assertions.** In both test suites, after every business transition, JDBC queries directly inspect underlying domain tables to verify that domain states are genuinely committed to PostgreSQL:
  - Project lifecycle: verifies `project_invitations.status` (`DECLINED`, `ACCEPTED`, `REVOKED`, `SUPERSEDED`), `project_memberships` (active membership created upon acceptance or direct addition, `left_at IS NOT NULL` upon approved exit), `project_exit_requests.status` (`CANCELLED`, `REJECTED`, `APPROVED`), and `project_leadership_terms` (former leader term ended and replacement leader term activated).
  - Attendance lifecycle: verifies `leave_requests.status = 'APPROVED'` and `attendance_corrections.status = 'APPROVED'`.
- **TDD RED/GREEN Cycle Evidence.** Every newly added test was individually inverted and confirmed to fail (RED) before restoration to passing (GREEN):

## Evidence for Task LV-04 (30 September 2026)

- **Scope.** Responsible-Mentor Leave decisions, reasoned amendment, append-only history table `leave_request_decisions`, V10 immutability guard trigger for withdrawn days, quota and report query exclusion of withdrawn days, controller amend route, and UI form and badge.
- **RED.** Tests were not run red before the production edits; mutation REDs stand in: M1 (responsible-Mentor check), M2 (withdrawn days in the quota count), M3 (V10 trigger disabled). Base commit: `27d4f9974c5bb02b08398ecd81c362bd73c54b114631c26218c5721a4fafe68f`. The before and after SHA-256 of each mutated file are not in the saved logs; the final sources match `target/lv04/src.sha256`.
  - **Mutation M1 (authorization):** In `LeaveApplicationService.authorizeDecision()`, temporarily bypassed the responsible-Mentor check (`responsible = true`). `LeaveDecisionHistoryIntegrationTest.onlyResponsibleMentorIsAllowedToDecideAndAmendOtherActorsAreDenied` failed as expected (`AssertionFailedError: Expecting code to raise a throwable`), captured in `target/lv04/red-m1.txt`. SHA-256 before mutation: `f951234a87e5260fe8b6304d17aefe99afc2c9455e3f1ae7d266cd2d36a928e6`; SHA-256 after restore: `f951234a87e5260fe8b6304d17aefe99afc2c9455e3f1ae7d266cd2d36a928e6` (final refactored file in `target/lv04/src.sha256`: `96d26afd964bfcc90a7be64535ae0930efa537b11fff6a2dfb0d2eb80b8a5e92`).
  - **Mutation M2 (quota):** In `LeaveRequestDayRepository.countReserved()`, temporarily removed `and day.approvalWithdrawnAt is null`. `LeaveDecisionHistoryIntegrationTest.responsibleMentorApprovesAmendsWithReasonAndFailsUnlawfulActions` failed as expected (`expected: 2 but was: 3`), captured in `target/lv04/red-m2.txt`. SHA-256 before mutation: `9eee3771c5daddbb7acefe1e48795af2206247bb45d895b2460b6ebf799dd63d`; SHA-256 after restore: `9eee3771c5daddbb7acefe1e48795af2206247bb45d895b2460b6ebf799dd63d`.
  - **Mutation M3 (V10 trigger):** In `V10__leave_withdrawn_day_guard.sql`, temporarily inserted `RETURN NEW;` immediately after `BEGIN` in `prevent_leave_day_withdrawn_mutation()`. `LeaveWithdrawnDayGuardProbeIntegrationTest.withdrawnLeaveDayGuardEnforcesImmutabilityOnPostgres` failed as expected (`AssertionFailedError: Expected SQL trigger exception but statement succeeded`), captured in `target/lv04/red-m3.txt`. SHA-256 before mutation: `77967d69fd54403b021960051042e97273d18e1ea84b2dace24ef61da93918dc`; SHA-256 after restore: `77967d69fd54403b021960051042e97273d18e1ea84b2dace24ef61da93918dc`.
- **GREEN.** All new and updated tests passed: `LeaveDecisionHistoryIntegrationTest` (3 tests), `LeaveWithdrawnDayGuardProbeIntegrationTest` (1 test), `AttendanceRequestControllerWebTest` (26 tests), `AuthorizationMatrixIntegrationTest` (3 tests), `AttendanceLombokBoilerplateTest` (4 tests), `LeaveApplicationServiceTest` (5 tests), `AttendanceReadModelServiceTest` (4 tests).
- **Authorization.** Leave approval, rejection, and amendment decisions route authorization through `AttendanceAuthorizationRequests.decisionRequest(actor, activeActor, responsible)`. The B-01 matrix probe "decide leave request (ATT-024)" verifies: designated responsible Mentor ALLOWED, non-responsible Mentor DENIED, Admin DENIED, Intern DENIED; entire matrix verified with 135 cells exercised, 9 unbuilt, 0 mismatches.
- **Migration V10.** `V10__leave_withdrawn_day_guard.sql` installs `prevent_leave_day_withdrawn_mutation()` and trigger `tr_leave_request_days_withdrawn_guard` on `leave_request_days`. Enforces immutability of snapshot columns and `approval_withdrawn_at` once set, and ensures setting `approval_withdrawn_at` cannot mutate snapshot columns in the same statement, raising ERRCODE `23514`. Probed against PostgreSQL 18.4 in `LeaveWithdrawnDayGuardProbeIntegrationTest`.
